\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}=f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})=\sigma(\underbrace{\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}}_{\mathbf{Z}^{(\ell)}})\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{\ell}},

(39)

and its gradient w.r.t. the input node embedding matrix
\mathbf{D}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{\ell-1}}
is computed as

\mathbf{D}^{(\ell)}=\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})=[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top},

(40)

and its gradient w.r.t. the weight matrix
\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{\ell-1}\times d_{\ell}}
is computed as

\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}=\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})=[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\Big].

(41)

Similarly, we can formulate the calculation of node embedding matrix
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{\ell}}
at each GCN layer in
SGCN
as

\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}=\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})=\sigma(\underbrace{\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}}_{\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}}),

(42)

and its gradient w.r.t. the input node embedding matrix
\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{\ell-1}}
is computed as

\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}=\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})=[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big],

(43)

and its gradient w.r.t. the weight matrix
\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{\ell-1}\times d_{\ell}}
is computed as

\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}=\Big[\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}):=[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\Big].

(44)

Let us denote the gradient of loss w.r.t. the final node embedding matrix as

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\mathbf{H}^{(L)},\mathbf{y})}{\partial\mathbf{H}^{(L)}}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{L}},[\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}]_{i}=\frac{1}{N}\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\mathbf{h}_{i}^{(L)},y_{i})}{\partial\mathbf{h}_{i}^{(L)}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{L}},

(45)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)},\mathbf{y})}{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{L}},[\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}]_{i}=\frac{1}{B}\mathbf{1}_{\{i\in\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}}\}}\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\widetilde{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{(L)},y_{i})}{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{(L)}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{L}}.

Notice that
\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}
is a
N\times d_{L}
matrix with the row number
i\in\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}}
are non-zero vectors.
Then we can write the gradient for the
\ell
th weight matrix in
FullGCN
and
SGCN
as

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}=\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}),

(46)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}=\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}).

B.2
Upper bounds on the node embedding matrices and layerwise gradients

Based on the Assumption
4.2
, we first derive the upper-bound on the node embedding matrices and the gradient passing from
\ell
th layer node embedding matrix to the
(\ell-1)
th layer node embedding matrix.

Proposition 1

For any
\ell\in[L]
, the Frobenius norm of node embedding matrices, gradient passing from the
\ell
th layer node embeddings to the
(\ell-1)
th are bounded

\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq B_{H},~\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq B_{H},~\|\frac{\partial\sigma(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})}{\partial\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq B_{D},~\|\frac{\partial\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})}{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq B_{D},

(47)

where

B_{H}=\max\Big\{1,(C_{\sigma}B_{LA}B_{W})^{L}\Big\}B_{X},~B_{D}=\max\Big\{1,(B_{LA}C_{\sigma}B_{W})^{L-\ell}\Big\}C_{\text{loss}}

(48)

Proof

Let first upper bounded the node embeddings,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|\sigma(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(49)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}B_{LA}B_{W}\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq(C_{\sigma}B_{LA}B_{W})^{\ell}\|\mathbf{X}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{1,(C_{\sigma}B_{LA}B_{W})^{L}\Big\}B_{X}.

Then, we derive the upper bound of gradient passing between layers,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\Big\|[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(50)

\displaystyle\leq B_{LA}C_{\sigma}B_{W}\|\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\Big(B_{LA}C_{\sigma}B_{W}\Big)^{L-\ell}\|\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{1,(B_{LA}C_{\sigma}B_{W})^{L}\Big\}C_{\text{loss}}.

A similar strategy can be used to derive the upper bound of its stochastic version.

B.3
Lipschitz continuity and smoothness property of graph convolution layers

Then, we derive the Lipschitz continuity of
\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\cdot,\cdot)
and its gradient. Notice that the following result hold for deterministic function
f^{(\ell)}(\cdot,\cdot)
as well.

Proposition 2

\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\cdot,\cdot)
is
C_{H}
-Lipschitz continuous w.r.t. the input node embedding matrix where
C_{H}=C_{\sigma}B_{LA}B_{W}
.

Proof

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(51)

\displaystyle=\|\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}B_{LA}B_{W}\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Proposition 3

\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\cdot,\cdot)
is
C_{W}
-Lipschitz continuous w.r.t. the weight matrix where
C_{W}=C_{\sigma}B_{LA}B_{H}
.

Proof

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})-\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(52)

\displaystyle=\|\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})-\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}B_{LA}B_{H}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Proposition 4

\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)
is
L_{H}
-Lipschitz continuous where

L_{H}=\max\Big\{B_{LA}C_{\sigma}B_{W},B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma},B_{LA}B_{D}C_{\sigma}+B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}B_{W}L_{\sigma}B_{H}\Big\}.

(53)

Proof

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(54)

\displaystyle\leq\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq B_{LA}C_{\sigma}B_{W}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(55)

\displaystyle\leq\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(56)

\displaystyle\leq\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq(B_{LA}B_{D}C_{\sigma}+B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}B_{W}L_{\sigma}B_{H})\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Proposition 5

\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)
is
L_{W}
-Lipschitz continuous where

L_{W}=\max\Big\{B_{LA}B_{H}C_{\sigma},B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}H_{D}L_{\sigma},B_{LA}B_{D}C_{\sigma}+B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}B_{D}L_{\sigma}\Big\}.

(57)

Proof

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(58)

\displaystyle\leq\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)

\displaystyle-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq B_{LA}B_{H}C_{\sigma}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{1},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{2},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(59)

\displaystyle\leq\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)

\displaystyle-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq(B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}+B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}B_{D}L_{\sigma}B_{W})\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(60)

\displaystyle\leq\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})\Big)

\displaystyle-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}H_{D}L_{\sigma}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

B.4
Lipschitz continouity of the gradient of graph convolutional network

Let first recall the parameters and gradients of a
L
-layer GCN is defined as

\bm{\theta}=\{\mathbf{W}^{(1)},\ldots,\mathbf{W}^{(L)}\},~\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})=\{\mathbf{G}^{(1)},\ldots,\mathbf{G}^{(L)}\},

(61)

where
\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}
is defined as the gradient w.r.t. the
\ell
th layer weight matrix. Let us slightly abuse of notation and define the distance between two set of parameters
\bm{\theta}_{1},\bm{\theta}_{2}
and its gradient as

\|\bm{\theta}_{1}-\bm{\theta}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}},~\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})-\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{2})\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{G}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(62)

Then, we derive the Lipschitz continuous constant of the gradient of a
L
-layer graph convolutonal network.
Notice that the above result also hold for sampling-based GCN training.

Lemma 1

The gradient of an
L
-layer GCN is
L_{\mathrm{F}}
-Lipschitz continuous with
L_{\mathrm{F}}=L(LU_{\max L}^{2}U_{\max C}^{2}+U_{\max L}^{2})
, i.e.,

\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})-\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{2})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq L_{\mathrm{F}}\|\bm{\theta}_{1}-\bm{\theta}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

(63)

where

\displaystyle U_{\max C}

\displaystyle=\max\{1,C_{H}\}^{L}C_{W},~

(64)

\displaystyle U_{\max L}

\displaystyle=L_{W}\max\{1,L_{H}\}^{L}\times\max\{1,L_{\text{loss}}\}.

Proof

We first consider the gradient w.r.t. the
\ell
th graph convolutional layer weight matrix

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(65)

\displaystyle=\|\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{1},\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{2},\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{1},\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{2},\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{2},\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{2},\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{2},\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{2},\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\ldots

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{2}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{2}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{2}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{2}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

By the Lipschitz continuity of
\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\cdot)
and
\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\cdot)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq L_{W}L_{H}^{L-\ell-1}L_{\text{loss}}\|\mathbf{H}^{(L)}_{1}-\mathbf{H}^{(L)}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(66)

\displaystyle+L_{W}L_{H}^{L-\ell-1}(\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(L-1)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(L-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(L)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(L)}\|_{\mathrm{F}})+\ldots

\displaystyle+L_{W}L_{H}(\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle+L_{W}(\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}).

Let define
U_{\max L}
as

U_{\max L}=L_{W}\cdot\max\{1,L_{H}\}^{L}\cdot\max\{1,L_{\text{loss}}\},

(67)

then we can rewrite the above equation as

\|\mathbf{G}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq U_{\max L}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\Big)+U_{\max L}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\Big).

(68)

Then, let consider the upper bound of
\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|f^{(\ell)}(f^{(\ell-1)}(\ldots f^{(1)}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(1)})\ldots,\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}),\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})

(69)

\displaystyle-f^{(\ell)}(f^{(\ell-1)}(\ldots f^{(1)}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(1)})\ldots,\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}),\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|f^{(\ell)}(f^{(\ell-1)}(\ldots f^{(1)}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(1)})\ldots,\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}),\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-f^{(\ell)}(f^{(\ell-1)}(\ldots f^{(1)}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(1)})\ldots,\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell-1)}),\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\ldots

\displaystyle+\|f^{(\ell)}(f^{(\ell-1)}(\ldots f^{(1)}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(1)})\ldots,\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}),\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-f^{(\ell)}(f^{(\ell-1)}(\ldots f^{(1)}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(1)})\ldots,\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell-1)}),\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

By the Lipschitz continuity of
f^{(\ell)}(\cdot,\cdot)
we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{H}^{L-1}C_{W}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(1)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\ldots+C_{W}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(70)

\displaystyle\leq U_{\max C}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\Big),

where
U_{\max C}=\max\{1,C_{H}\}^{L}C_{W}
.

Plugging it back we have

\|\mathbf{G}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(LU_{\max L}U_{\max C}+U_{\max L})\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\Big).

(71)

Summing both size from
\ell=1
to
\ell=L
we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})-\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{2})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{G}_{1}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}_{2}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(72)

\displaystyle\leq L(LU_{\max L}U_{\max C}+U_{\max L})\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq L(LU_{\max L}U_{\max C}+U_{\max L})\|\bm{\theta}_{1}-\bm{\theta}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

B.5
Mean-square error of stochastic gradient

By bias-variance decomposition, we can decompose the mean-square error of stochastic gradient as

\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]=\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\Big[\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{\text{bias}~\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{b}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{\text{variance}~\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{n}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}\Big].

(73)

Therefore, we have to explicitly define the computation of
\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]
, which requires computing
\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}=\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}]
,
\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}=\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}]
, and
\bar{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}=\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]
.

Let defined a general form of the sampled Laplacian matrix
\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times N}
as

\widetilde{L}_{i,j}^{(\ell)}=\begin{cases}\frac{L_{i,j}}{\alpha_{i,j}}&\text{ if }i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}~\text{and}~j\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell-1)}\\
0&\text{ otherwise }\end{cases},

(74)

where
\alpha_{i,j}
is the weighted constant depends on the sampling algorithms.

The expectation of
\widetilde{L}_{i,j}^{(\ell)}
is computed as

\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{L}_{i,j}^{(\ell)}]=\mathbb{E}_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}}\Big[\mathbb{E}_{j\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell-1)}}[\widetilde{L}_{i,j}^{(\ell)}~|~i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}]\Big].

(75)

In order to compute the expectation of
SGCN
’s node embedding matrices,
let define the propagation matrix
\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times N}
as

P_{i,j}^{(\ell)}=\mathbb{E}_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}}\Big[\widetilde{L}_{i,j}^{(\ell)}~|~i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}\Big],

(76)

where the expectation is taken over row indices
i
. The above equation implies that under the condition that knowing the
i
th node is in
\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}
, we have
P_{i,j}^{(\ell)}=\widetilde{L}_{i,j},~\forall j=\{1,\ldots,N\}
. Let consider the mean-aggregation for the
i
th node as

\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(\ell)}=\sigma\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\widetilde{L}^{(\ell)}_{i,j}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(\ell-1)}\Big).

(77)

Then, under the condition
i
th node is in
\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}
, we can replace
\widetilde{L}^{(\ell)}_{i,j}
by
P^{(\ell)}_{i,j}
, which gives us

\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(\ell)}=\sigma\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}P^{(\ell)}_{i,j}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(\ell-1)}\Big)

(78)

As a result, we can write the expectation of
\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(\ell)}
with respect to the indices
i
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}}[\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(\ell)}~|~i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}}\Big[\sigma\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\widetilde{L}^{(\ell)}_{i,j}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(\ell-1)}\Big)~|~i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}\Big]

(79)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}}\Big[\sigma\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}P^{(\ell)}_{i,j}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(\ell-1)}\Big)~|~i\in\mathcal{B}^{(\ell)}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sigma\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}P^{(\ell)}_{i,j}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(\ell-1)}\Big).

Then define
\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{\ell}}
as the node embedding of using full-batch but a subset of neighbors for neighbor aggregation, i.e.,

\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}=\sigma(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}),

(80)

where all rows in
\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}
are non-zero vectors.

Using the notations defined above, we can compute
\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{L}}
,
\bar{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{\ell-1}\times d_{\ell}}
, and
\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{\ell-1}}
as

\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}=\mathbb{E}\Big[\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)})}{\partial\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}}\Big]\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times d_{L}},\bar{\mathbf{d}}_{i}=\frac{1}{N}\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\bar{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{(L)},y_{i})}{\partial\bar{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{(L)}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{L}},

(81)

and

\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}=\Big[\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})=[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big],

(82)

and

\bar{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}=\Big[\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})=[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\Big].

(83)

As a result, we can represent
\bar{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}=\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]
as

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}=\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})).

(84)

B.6
Supporting lemmas

We derive the upper-bound of the bias and variance of the stochastic gradient in the following lemmas.

Lemma 2
(Upper-bound on variance)

We can upper-bound the variance of stochastic gradient in
SGCN
as

\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])

(85)

Proof

By definition, we can write the variance in
SGCN
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(86)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}),\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\ldots

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+2)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+2)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(L+1)L_{W}^{2}L_{H}^{2(L-\ell-1)}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)L_{W}^{2}L_{H}^{2(L-\ell-2)}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\ldots

\displaystyle+(L+1)L_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+2)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+2)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

From the previous equation, we know that there are three key factors that will affect the variance:

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the last layer node representations

\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(87)

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the input node embedding matrix at each graph convolutional layer

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+2)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+2)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(88)

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the weight matrix at each graph convolutional layer

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(89)

First, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
87
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)},\mathbf{y})}{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}}-\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)},\mathbf{y})}{\partial\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right]

(90)

\displaystyle\leq L_{loss}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq L_{loss}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(L)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-\sigma(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq L_{loss}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(L)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}-\mathbf{P}^{(L)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq L_{loss}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}B_{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(L)}-\mathbf{P}^{(L)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

Then, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
88
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(91)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}-[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}[\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}-[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{4}L_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{H}^{2}B_{W}^{4}L_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}+\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\Big(B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{H}^{2}B_{W}^{4}L_{\sigma}^{2}+2B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\Big)\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+4B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Finally, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
89
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(92)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)-[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}[\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)-[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\Big(B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}+B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\Big)\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2B_{D}^{2}B_{H}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\Big(B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{4}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}+B_{H}^{2}B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\Big)\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2B_{D}^{2}B_{H}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Combining the result from Eq.
87
,
88
,
89
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(L)}-\mathbf{P}^{(L)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])

(93)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(L)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Lemma 3
(Upper-bound on bias)

We can upper-bound the bias of stochastic gradient in
SGCN
as

\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\mathcal{O}(\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}).

(94)

Proof

By definition, we can write the bias of stochastic gradient in
SGCN
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(95)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\ldots

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(L+1)L_{W}^{2}L_{H}^{2(L-\ell-1)}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)L_{W}^{2}L_{H}^{2(L-\ell-2)}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\ldots

\displaystyle+(L+1)L_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

From the previous equation, we know that there are three key factors that will affect the bias:

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the last layer node representations

\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(96)

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the input node embedding matrix at each graph convolutional layer

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(97)

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the weight matrix at each graph convolutional layer

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(98)

Firstly, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
96
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)},\mathbf{y})}{\partial\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}}-\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\mathbf{H}^{(L)},\mathbf{y})}{\partial\mathbf{H}^{(L)}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(99)

\displaystyle\leq L_{loss}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}-\mathbf{H}^{(L)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

The upper-bound for
\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\sigma(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\sigma(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(100)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}+\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}B_{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}B_{LA}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(1)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Therefore, we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(1)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(L)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

(101)

Then, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
97
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(102)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}-[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{4}L_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}+\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{4}L_{\sigma}^{2}B_{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2B_{LA}^{4}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{4}L_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(1)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Finally, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
98
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(103)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)-[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)-[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}[\|[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)-[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{LA}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}B_{D}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}+\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\Big(B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{LA}^{2}+B_{LA}^{4}B_{H}^{2}B_{D}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\Big)\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{4}B_{D}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(1)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Combining the result from Eq.
96
,
97
,
98
we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}]-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(1)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(L)}-\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

(104)

B.7
Remaining steps toward Theorem
4.1

By the smoothness of
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bm{\theta}_{t}\rangle+\frac{L_{f}}{2}\|\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bm{\theta}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(105)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\eta\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\rangle+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{f}}{2}\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

Let
\mathcal{F}_{t}=\{\{\mathcal{B}_{1}^{(\ell)}\}_{\ell=1}^{L},\ldots,\{\mathcal{B}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\}_{\ell=1}^{L}\}
.
Note that the weight parameters
\bm{\theta}_{t}
is a function of history of the generated random process and hence is random.
Taking expectation on both sides condition on
\mathcal{F}_{t}
and using
\eta<1/L_{f}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t+1})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

(106)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\eta\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\mathbb{E}[\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\rangle+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{f}}{2}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\mathbb{E}[\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\eta\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{b}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\rangle+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{f}}{2}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{n}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]+\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{b}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\frac{\eta}{2}\Big(-2\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{b}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\rangle+\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{b}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\Big)+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{f}}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{n}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\frac{\eta}{2}\Big(-\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\Big)+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{f}}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{n}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

Denote
\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}
as the upper bound of bias of stochasitc gradient as shown in Lemma
3
and Denote
\Delta_{\mathbf{n}}
as the upper bound of bias of stochastic gradient as shown in Lemma
2
.
Plugging in the upper bound of bias and variance, taking expectation over
\mathcal{F}_{t}
, and rearranging the term we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\frac{2}{\eta}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})]-\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t+1})]\Big)+\eta L_{f}\Delta_{\mathbf{n}}+\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}

(107)

Summing up from
t=1
to
T
, rearranging we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})]-\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t+1})])+\eta L_{f}\Delta_{\mathbf{n}}+\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}

(108)

\displaystyle\underset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{2}{\eta T}(\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})-\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star}))+\eta L_{f}\Delta_{\mathbf{n}}+\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}

where the inequality
(a)
is due to
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star})\leq\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{T+1})]
.

By selecting learning rate as
\eta=1/\sqrt{T}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})-\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star}))}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{L_{f}\Delta_{\mathbf{n}}}{\sqrt{T}}+\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}

(109)

Appendix C
Proof of Theorem
5.1

C.1
Upper-bounded on the node embedding matrices and layerwise gradients

When using variance reduction algorithm, we cannot use upper bound on the node embedding matrices and layerwise gradients as derived in Proposition
1
.
To see this, let consider the upper bound on the node embedding matrix before the activation function
\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}+\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(110)

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

Therefore, in the worst case, the norm of node embedding matrices is growing as the inner loop size. To control the growth on matrix norm, we introduced an early stopping criterion by checking the norm of node embeddings, and immediately start another snapshot step if the condition is triggered.

Let define
t_{s}
as the stopping time of the inner loop in the
s
th outer loop,

e_{s}=e_{s-1}+\min\Big\{\max_{k\geq e_{s-1}}\{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\alpha\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\},K\Big\},~t_{0}=1,

(111)

and
E_{s}=e_{s}-e_{s-1}\leq K
as the snapshot gap at the
s
th inner-loop and
S
as the total number of outer loops.
By doing so, we can upper bounded the node embedding matrix by
\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\alpha B_{H},~\forall t\in[T]
.

C.2
Supporting lemmas

In the following lemma, we derive the upper-bound on the node embedding approximation error of each GCN layer in
SGCN+
.
This upper-bound plays an important role in the analysis of the upper-bound of the bias term for the stochastic gradient.
Suppose the input node embedding matrix for the
\ell
GCN layer as
\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}
, the forward propagation for the
\ell
th layer in
SGCN+
is defined as

\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}=\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})=\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}_{t}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t},

(112)

and the forward propagation for the
\ell
th layer in
FullGCN
is defined as

f^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})=\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}.

(113)

In the following, we derive the upper-bound of

\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})-f^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(114)

Lemma 4

Let consider the
s
th epoch, let
t
be the current step. Therefore, for any
t\in\{e_{s-1},\ldots,e_{s}\}
, we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\eta^{2}E_{s}\times\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{4}\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}\big|\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(j)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\big|\right).

(115)

Proof

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(116)

\displaystyle=\|[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]+[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]-[\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\langle\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\rangle

\displaystyle-2\langle\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)},\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\rangle

\displaystyle-2\langle\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)},\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\rangle.

Recall that by the update rule, we have

\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}=\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)},

(117)

and

\mathbb{E}[\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]=\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}.

(118)

Taking expectation on both side of Eq.
116
condition on
\mathcal{F}_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

(119)

\displaystyle-\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

Then take the expectation on both side of Eq.
116
w.r.t.
\mathcal{F}_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(120)

\displaystyle-\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

Since we know
t\in\{e_{s-1},\ldots,e_{s}\}
, we can denote
t=e_{s-1}+k,~k\leq E_{s}
such that formulate Eq.
120
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(121)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(A)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\Big).

Knowing that we are using all neighbors at the snapshot step
t\in\{e_{0},\ldots,e_{S}\}
, we have
(A)=0
in Eq.
121
. As a result, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(122)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\Big(\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}_{(B)}\Big).

Let take a closer look at term
(B)
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(123)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(\ell)}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\Big)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(C)}.

Let take a closer look at term
(C)
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(124)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\underset{(a)}{\leq}2\alpha^{2}B_{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}],

where
(a)
is due to the early stop criterion in our algorithm.

By induction, we can formulate Eq.
124
by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(125)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha^{2}B_{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2^{2}\alpha^{2}B_{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\ldots

\displaystyle+2^{\ell}\alpha^{2}B_{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(1)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

By the update rule of weight matrices, we know

\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]=\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{G}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(126)

By the definition of
\bar{\mathbf{G}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}
in Eq.
84
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{G}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{2}B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}.

(127)

Plugging the results back, we have the upper bound of Eq.
121
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}-\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(128)

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}E_{s}\times\mathcal{O}\Big(\alpha^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}|\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\times\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}E_{s}\times\mathcal{O}\Big(\alpha^{4}\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}|\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big).

Based on the upper-bound of node embedding approximation error of each graph convolutional layer, we derived the upper-bound on the bias of stochastic gradient in
SGCN
.

Lemma 5
(Upper-bound on bias)

Let consider the
s
th epoch, let
t
be the current step. Therefore, for any
t\in\{e_{s-1},\ldots,e_{s}\}
,
we can upper-bound the bias of stochastic gradient in
SGCN+
as

\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}]-\mathbf{G}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\eta^{2}E_{s}\times\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{4}\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}|\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\right)

(129)

Proof

From the decomposition of bias as shown in previously in Eq.
95
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}]-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(130)

\displaystyle\leq(L+1)L_{W}^{2}L_{H}^{2(L-\ell-1)}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)L_{W}^{2}L_{H}^{2(L-\ell-2)}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(L+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(L)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\ldots

\displaystyle+(L+1)L_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+2)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

From the previous equation, we know that there are three key factors that will affect the bias:

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the last layer node representations

\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(131)

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the input node embedding matrix at each graph convolutional layer

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)}_{t},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)}_{t},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(132)

•

The difference of gradient with respect to the weight matrix at each graph convolutional layer

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(133)

Firstly, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
131
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}_{t},\mathbf{y})}{\partial\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}_{t}}-\frac{\partial\text{Loss}(\mathbf{H}^{(L)}_{t},\mathbf{y})}{\partial\mathbf{H}^{(L)}_{t}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(134)

\displaystyle\leq L_{loss}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{H}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq L_{loss}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

We can decompose
\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(135)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2B_{LA}^{2}B_{W}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L-1)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Using result from Lemma
4
, we can upper bound Eq.
134
by

\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\eta^{2}E_{s}\times\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{4}\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\Big|\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\Big|\right).

(136)

Then, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
132
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(137)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}-[\mathbf{L}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\Big)[\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(A)},

where
\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}=\bar{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{P}^{(\ell)}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}
.

Let take a closer look at term
(A)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(138)

\displaystyle\leq B_{LA}^{2}B_{W}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{LA}^{2}B_{W}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2B_{LA}^{2}B_{W}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

Therefore, by induction we can upper bound Eq.
137
by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(139)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(2)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(2)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(1)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Using result from Lemma
4
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t},\bar{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)}_{t},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(140)

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}E_{s}\times\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{4}\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}|\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\right).

Finally, let consider the upper-bound of Eq.
133
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(141)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t})\Big)-[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|[\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t})\Big)-[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}[\|[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t})\Big)-[\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}]^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{L}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{D}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{LA}^{2}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(B)}

\displaystyle+4B_{LA}^{2}\alpha^{2}B_{H}^{2}B_{D}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\Big).

By definition, we can write the term
(B)
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(142)

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-2)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+2C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-2)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-2)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

Plugging term
(B)
back and using Eq.
138
and Lemma
4
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\bar{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\bar{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(143)

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}E_{s}\times\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{4}\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}|\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\right).

Combining the result from Eq.
131
,
132
,
133
we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}]-\mathbf{G}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\eta^{2}E_{s}\times\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{4}\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}|\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{P}_{t}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\right).

(144)

C.3
Remaining steps toward Theorem
5.1

Now we are ready to prove Theorem
5.1
.
By the smoothness of
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bm{\theta}_{t}\rangle+\frac{L_{\mathrm{F}}}{2}\|\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bm{\theta}_{t}\|^{2}

(145)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\eta\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\rangle+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{\mathrm{F}}}{2}\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|^{2}.

Let
\mathcal{F}_{t}=\{\{\mathcal{B}_{1}^{(\ell)}\}_{\ell=1}^{L},\ldots,\{\mathcal{B}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\}_{\ell=1}^{L}\}
.
Note that the weight parameters
\bm{\theta}_{t}
is a function of history of the generated random process and hence is random.
Taking expectation on both sides condition on
\mathcal{F}_{t}
and using
\eta<1/L_{\mathrm{F}}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t+1})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

(146)
