\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\eta\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\mathbb{E}[\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\rangle+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{\mathrm{F}}}{2}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\mathbb{E}[\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\eta\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{b}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\rangle+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{\mathrm{F}}}{2}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{n}_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]+\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{b}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\frac{\eta}{2}\Big(-2\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{b}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\rangle+\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{b}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\|^{2}\Big)+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{\mathrm{F}}}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{n}_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\frac{\eta}{2}\Big(-\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\Big)+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{\mathrm{F}}}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{n}_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}].

Plugging in the upper bound of bias and variance, taking expectation over
\mathcal{F}_{t}
, and rearranging the term we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|^{2}]\leq\frac{2}{\eta}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})]-\mathbb{E}[\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t+1})]\Big)+\eta L_{\mathrm{F}}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{n}_{t}\|^{2}]+\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}].

(147)

Summing up from
t=1
to
T
, rearranging we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})]-\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t+1})])+\eta L_{\mathrm{F}}\Delta_{\mathbf{n}}+\eta^{2}\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}^{+\prime}

(148)

\displaystyle\underset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{2}{\eta T}(\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})-\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star}))+\eta L_{\mathrm{F}}\Delta_{\mathbf{n}}+\eta^{2}\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}^{+\prime},

where the inequality
(a)
is due to
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star})\leq\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{T+1})]
.

By selecting learning rate as
\eta=1/\sqrt{T}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})-\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star}))}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{L_{\mathrm{F}}\Delta_{\mathbf{n}}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}^{+\prime}}{T}.

(149)

C.4
Discussion on the elimination of bias term

Recall that the existence of bias term
\Delta_{b}
in Theorem 1 is due to inner-layer sampling.
In the analysis of
SGCN
, since the bias term
\Delta_{b}
is not multiplied by the learning rate
\eta
(Eq. 110 - 111), the bias term is not decreasing as the
T
increases when selecting
\eta=1/\sqrt{T}
.
To resolve this non-decreasing bias term,
SGCN+
proposes to use historical node embeddings for variance reduction, such that the bias term
\Delta_{b}^{+}
is controlled by the difference between the current model to the snapshot model. Because the difference between the current model to the snapshot model is affected by the selection of
\eta
(Lemma 4), in the analysis of
SGCN+
, the bias term
\Delta_{b}^{+}
is multiplied by
\eta^{2}
(Eq. 151). Therefore, by selecting
\eta=1/\sqrt{T}
the bias term is decreasing as the number of iterations increases.

Appendix D
Proof of Theorem
6.1

D.1
Upper-bounded on the node embedding matrices and layerwise gradients

When using variance reduction algorithm, we cannot use upper bound on the node embedding matrices and layerwise gradients as derived in Proposition
1
.

To see this, let consider the upper bound on the node embedding matrix before the activation function
\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}+\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(150)

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Similarly, we can compute the upper bound on the gradient passing from the
\ell+1
to the
\ell
th layer as

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t})\Big)[\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}]-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1})\Big)[\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(151)

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t})\Big)[\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}]-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]^{\top}\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1})\Big)[\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Similar to the discussion we had in the proof of Theorem
5.1
, in the worst case, the matrix norm are growing as the inner loop size. To control the growth on matrix norm, we introduced an early stopping criterion by checking the norm of node embeddings and gradient, and immediately start another snapshot step if the condition is triggered.

Let define
t_{s}
as the stopping time of the inner loop in the
s
th outer loop,

e_{s}=e_{s-1}+\min\Big\{\max_{k\geq e_{s-1}}\{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\alpha\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}~\text{and}~\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{k}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\beta\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\},K\Big\},~t_{0}=1,

(152)

and
E_{s}=e_{s}-e_{s-1}\leq K
as the snapshot gap at the
s
th inner-loop and
S
as the total number of outer loops.
By doing so, we can upper bounded the node embedding matrix by
\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\alpha B_{H},\|\mathbf{D}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\beta B_{D},~\forall t\in[T]
.

D.2
Supporting lemmas

In the following lemma, we decompose the mean-square error of stochastic gradient at the
\ell
th layer
\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\|F^{2}]
as the summation of

•

The difference between the gradient with respect to the last layer node embedding matrix

\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(153)

•

The difference of gradient passing from the
(\ell+1)
th layer node embedding to the
\ell
th layer node embedding

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(154)

•

The difference of gradient passing from the
\ell
th layer node embedding to the
\ell
th layer weight matrix

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(155)

Lemma 6

The mean-square error of stochastic gradient at the
\ell
th layer can be decomposed as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\|F^{2}]

(156)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Proof

By definition, we can write down the mean-square error of stochastic gradient as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(157)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}),\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\ldots\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\ldots,\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\ldots

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}),\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+(L+1)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Recall the definition of stochastic gradient for all model parameters
\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})=\{\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\}_{\ell=1}^{L}
where
\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}
is the gradient for the
\ell
th weight matrix, i.e.,

\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}=\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}-\eta\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}.

(158)

In the following lemma, we derive the upper-bound on the difference of the gradient passing from the
\ell
th to
(\ell-1)
th layer given the same inputs
\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},~\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}
, where the backward propagation for the
\ell
th layer in
SGCN++
is defined as

\displaystyle\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})

(159)

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}],

and the backward propagation for the
\ell
th layer in
FullGCN
is defined as

\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})=\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}.

(160)

Lemma 7

Let consider the
s
th epoch, let
t
be the current step. Therefore, for any
t\in\{e_{s-1},\ldots,e_{s}\}
, we can upper-bound the difference of the gradient with respect to the input node embedding matrix at the
\ell
th graph convolutional layer given the same input
\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}
and
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}
by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(161)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right)

Proof

To simplify the presentation, let us denote
\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}=\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})
. Then, by definition we have

\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}=\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}.

(162)

Therefore, we know that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(163)

\displaystyle=\|[\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]

\displaystyle+[\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]-[\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\underbrace{\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}}_{(A_{1})}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\underbrace{\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}}_{(A_{2})}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|\underbrace{\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}}_{(A_{3})}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\langle A_{1},A_{2}\rangle-2\langle A_{1},A_{3}\rangle-2\langle A_{2},A_{3}\rangle.

Taking expectation condition on
\mathcal{F}_{t}
on both side, and using the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

(164)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}.

Therefore, we can write Eq.
163
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

(165)

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

\displaystyle-\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

Then, taking expectation over
\mathcal{F}_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(166)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle-[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

Since we know
t\in\{e_{s-1},\ldots,e_{s}\}
, we can denote
t=e_{s-1}+k,~k\leq E_{s}
such that we can write Eq.
166
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(167)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}_{e_{s-1}+k}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle-[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\Big).

Knowing that we are taking full-batch gradient descent when
t=t_{s-1}
, we can write Eq.
167
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(168)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\Big(|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(B)}.

Let take closer look at term
(B)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(169)

\displaystyle\leq 3C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\underbrace{\|\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}_{(C_{1})}+3\beta^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(C_{2})}

\displaystyle+3\beta^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{D}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

For term
(C_{1})
by definition we know

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(170)

\displaystyle=\|\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+2)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\Big)-\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+2)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+2)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\Big)-\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+2)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+3\|\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+2)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\Big)-\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+2)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+3\|\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+2)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\Big)-\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+2)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\|\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+2)}-\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+2)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{2}\|\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{2}\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}).

By induction, we can upper bound
(C_{1})
in Eq.
169
by

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\mathcal{O}(\|\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})}_{(D_{1})}

(171)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}(\beta^{2}\|\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})}_{(D_{2})}+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{2}\|\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(L)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(L)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{2}\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{2}\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(L)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(L)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}).

For term
(D_{1})
in Eq.
171
we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})}{\partial\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(L)}}-\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})}{\partial\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(L)}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(172)

\displaystyle\leq L_{\text{loss}}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\|\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

For term
(D_{2})
in Eq.
171
we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}^{2}\|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(173)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{LA}^{2}\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}+\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{LA}^{2}\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{LA}^{2}\|\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\sigma}^{4}B_{LA}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\|\mathbf{Z}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\alpha^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}\|\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

By induction we can upper bound term
(D_{2})
in Eq.
171
by

\|\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}(\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{t}-\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}).

(174)

For term
(C_{2})
in Eq.
169
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(175)

\displaystyle\leq 2B_{LA}^{2}B_{W}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2\alpha^{2}B_{LA}^{2}B_{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

By induction term
(C_{2})
in Eq.
171

\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\mathcal{O}(\alpha^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots+\mathcal{O}(\alpha^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{t}-\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

(176)

Plugging
(D_{1}),(D_{2})
back to
(C_{1})
and
(C_{1}),(C_{2}),(C_{3})
back to
(B)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(177)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\Big(\mathcal{O}\big((\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(1)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\big)+\ldots

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\big((\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(L)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(L)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\big)\Big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\mathcal{O}\Big((\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\Big).

Then plugging term
(B)
back to Eq.
169
we conclude the proof.

Using the previous lemma, we provide the upper-bound of Eq.
154
, which is one of the three key factors that affect the mean-square error of stochastic gradient at the
\ell
th layer.

Lemma 8

Let suppose
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
.
The upper-bound on the difference of the gradient with respect to the input node embedding matrix at the
\ell
th graph convolutional layer given the same input
\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}
but different input
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}
is defined as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(178)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(j)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right)

Proof

For the gradient w.r.t. the node embedding matrices, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(179)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]\Big)

\displaystyle-\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\Big(\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]\Big)}_{(A)}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(A)}

\displaystyle+2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}\Big)-\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(B)}.

Let first take a closer look at term
(A)
in Eq.
179
.
Let suppose
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
. Then we can denote
t=e_{s-1}+k
for some
k\leq K
.
By Lemma
7
, term
(A)
can be bounded by

(A)\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].\right)

(180)

Then we take a closer look at term
(B)
in Eq.
179
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}\Big)-\Big(\mathbf{L}^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell)}))\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}\Big)\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(181)

\displaystyle\leq\beta^{2}B_{LA}^{2}B_{D}^{2}B_{W}^{2}L_{\sigma}^{2}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(C)}.

The term
(C)
in Eq.
181
can be decomposed as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(182)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+2B_{LA}^{2}B_{W}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

By induction, we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\mathcal{O}\Big(\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]}_{(D)}\Big)+\ldots+\mathcal{O}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(1)}-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\Big).

(183)

The upper-bound for term
(D)
is similar to one we have in the proof of Lemma
4
.

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(184)

\displaystyle=\|[\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}]+[\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]-[\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\langle\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t},\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\rangle-2\langle\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\rangle

\displaystyle-2\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\rangle.

Taking expectation condition on
\mathcal{F}_{t}
and using

\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]=\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1},

(185)

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]

(186)

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]-\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

Take expectation over
\mathcal{F}_{t}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(187)

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

Let suppose
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
. Then we can denote
t=e_{s-1}+k
for some
k\leq K
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(188)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{e_{s-1}+k}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}+k}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{e_{s-1}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{e_{s-1}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\underset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathcal{O}\Big(|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\times\alpha^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\Big).

where
(a)
follows the deviation of Eq.
128
.

Plugging
(D)
to
(C)
and
(C)
to
(B)
, we have

\displaystyle(B)\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right).

(189)

Plugging Eq.
180
and Eq.
189
back to Eq.
179
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(190)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right).

In the following lemma, we derive the upper-bound on the difference of the gradient with respect to the weight matrix at each graph convolutional layer. Suppose the input node embedding matrix for the
\ell
th GCN layer is defined as
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}
, the gradient calculated for the
\ell
th weight matrix in
SGCN++
is defined as

\displaystyle\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})

(191)

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)}_{t-1}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)})),

and the backward propagation for the
\ell
th layer in
FullGCN
is defined as

\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})=[\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)})).

(192)

Lemma 9

Let suppose
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
.
The upper-bound on the difference of the gradient with respect to the
\ell
th graph convolutional layer given the same input
\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}
and
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}
is defined as

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(193)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times(\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}+\alpha^{4}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right).

Proof

To simplify the presentation, let us denote
\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}=\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})
. Then, by definition, we have

\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}=\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))-[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)})).

(194)

Therefore, we know that

~\begin{aligned} &\|[\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))-\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\

&\leq\Big\|\underbrace{[\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{(\ell)}))-[\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))}_{(A_{1})}\Big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|\underbrace{[\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell-1)}]^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}\circ\sigma^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}))-\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}}_{A_{2}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|\underbrace{\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}}_{A_{3}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+2\langle A_{1},A_{2}\rangle-2\langle A_{1},A_{3}\rangle-2\langle A_{2},A_{3}\rangle.\end{aligned}

Lemma 10

Let suppose
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
.

\ell

Lemma 11

Let suppose
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
. Then the upper bound of

\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\times\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times\alpha^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right).

(214)

Proof

By definition we have

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\frac{\partial\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})}{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}_{t}}-\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})}{\partial\mathbf{H}^{(L)}_{t-1}}\Big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(215)

\displaystyle\leq L_{\text{loss}}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{H}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq L_{\text{loss}}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\underbrace{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}_{(A)}.

Let take closer look at term
(A)
:

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-f^{(L)}(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(216)

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-f^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\|f^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-f^{(L)}(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\|\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-f^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\|\sigma(\mathbf{L}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-\sigma(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{H}_{t}^{(L-1)}\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})-f^{(L)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(L-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(L)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2C_{\sigma}^{4}B_{LA}^{2}B_{W}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L-1)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L-1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

By induction, we have

\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\|\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-f^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right).

(217)

Let suppose
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
. Then we can denote
t=e_{s-1}+k
for some
k\leq K
.
From Eq.
188
, we know that

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-f^{(\ell)}(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(218)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathcal{O}\Big(|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\times\alpha^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\Big).

Therefore, we know

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}_{t}^{(L+1)}-\mathbf{D}_{t}^{(L+1)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(219)

\displaystyle\leq L_{\text{loss}}^{2}C_{\sigma}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(L)}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}^{(L)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times\alpha^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)}-\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\times\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times\alpha^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right).

which conclude the proof.

Combing the upper-bound of Eq.
153
,
154
,
155
, we provide the upper-bound of mean-suqare error of stochastic gradient in
SGCN++
.

Lemma 12

Let suppose
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
. Then we can denote
t=e_{s-1}+k
for some
k\leq K
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(220)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times(\alpha^{2}+1)(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right).

Proof

From Lemma
6
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}_{t}-\mathbf{G}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|F^{2}]

(221)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(L+1)}_{t}-\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(L-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(L)}_{t})-\nabla_{H}f^{(L)}(\mathbf{D}^{(L+1)}_{t},\mathbf{H}^{(L-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}^{(L)}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])+\ldots

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{H}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})-\nabla_{H}f^{(\ell+1)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+2)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell+1)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}])

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{W}\widetilde{f}^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})-\nabla_{W}f^{(\ell)}(\mathbf{D}^{(\ell+1)},\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)},\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]).

Plugging the result from support lemmas, i.e., Lemma
8
,
10
,
11
and using the definition of stochastic gradient for all model parameters
\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})=\{\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\}_{\ell=1}^{L}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(222)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times(\alpha^{2}+1)(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t-1})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right).

D.3
Remaining steps toward Theorem
6.1

By the smoothness of
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{T+1})

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})+\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bm{\theta}_{t}\rangle+\frac{L_{f}}{2}\|\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bm{\theta}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(223)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\eta\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t}),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\rangle+\frac{\eta^{2}L_{f}}{2}\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\underset{(a)}{=}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\frac{\eta}{2}\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{2}\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\Big(\frac{\eta}{2}-\frac{L_{f}\eta^{2}}{2}\Big)\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

where equality
(a)
is due to the fact
2\langle\bm{x},\bm{y}\rangle=\|\bm{x}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|\bm{y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
for any
\bm{x},\bm{y}
.

Take expectation on both sides, we have

\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{T+1})]\leq\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})]-\frac{\eta}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\frac{\eta}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\Big(\frac{\eta}{2}-\frac{L_{f}\eta^{2}}{2}\Big)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}].

(224)

By summing over
t=1,\ldots,T
where
T
is the inner-loop size, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(225)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})]-\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{T+1})]\Big)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}[\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\Big(1-L_{f}\eta\Big)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})]-\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star})]\Big)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}[\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\Big(1-L_{f}\eta\Big)\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]].

where
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star})
is the global optimal solution.

Let us consider each inner-loop with
s\in\{1,\ldots,S\}
and
t\in\{e_{s-1}+1,\ldots,e_{s}\}
, where
E_{s}=e_{s}-e_{s-1}
and
\sum_{s=1}^{S}E_{s}=T
.
Let define
C_{\alpha\beta}=(\alpha^{2}+1)(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\alpha^{2}\beta^{2})
.
Using Lemma
12
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{S}\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\Big(1-L_{f}\eta\Big)\sum_{s=1}^{S}\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

(226)

\displaystyle\underset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{s=1}^{S}\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\eta^{2}K\mathcal{O}\left(\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)\times C_{\alpha\beta}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta})_{t-1}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\right)

\displaystyle-\Big(1-L_{f}\eta\Big)\sum_{s=1}^{S}\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\left[\eta^{2}K\times\mathcal{O}\Big(C_{\alpha\beta}\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]-\|\mathbf{L}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}|\Big)-\Big(1-L_{f}\eta\Big)\right]\sum_{s=1}^{S}\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=[\eta^{2}\Delta_{\mathbf{b}+\mathbf{n}}^{++\prime}-(1-L_{f}\eta)]\sum_{s=1}^{S}\sum_{t=e_{s-1}+1}^{e_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}],

where
(a)
is due to
E_{s}\leq K
and Lemma
12
.

Notice that
\eta=\frac{2}{L_{f}+\sqrt{L_{f}^{2}+4\Delta_{\mathbf{b}+\mathbf{n}}^{++\prime}}}
is a root of equation
\eta^{2}\Delta_{\mathbf{b}+\mathbf{n}}^{++\prime}-(1-L_{f}\eta)=0
.
Therefore we have

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\frac{2}{\eta}\Big(\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})]-\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star})]\Big)

(227)

which implies

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{t})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]\leq\frac{1}{T}\Big(L_{f}+\sqrt{L_{f}^{2}+4\Delta_{\mathbf{b}+\mathbf{n}}^{++\prime}}\Big)\Big(\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}_{1})]-\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}^{\star})]\Big)

(228)

Appendix E
Connection to composite optimization

In this section, we formally compare the optimization problem in training GCNs to the standard composite optimization and highlight the key differences that necessitates developing a completely different variance reduction schema and convergence analysis compared to the composite optimization counterparts (e.g., see
fang2018spider
).

Different objective function.

In composite optimization, the output of the lower-level function is treated as the
parameter
of the outer-level function.
However in GCN, the output of the lower-level function is used as the
input
of the outer-level function, and the parameter of the outer-level function is independent of the output of the inner-layer result.

More specifically, a two-level composite optimization problem can be formulated as

F(\bm{\theta})=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}f_{i}\Big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j=1}^{M}g_{j}(\mathbf{w})\Big),~\bm{\theta}=\{\mathbf{w}\},

(229)

where
f_{i}(\cdot)
is the outer-level function computed on the
i
th data point,
g_{j}(\cdot)
is the inner-level function computed on the
j
th data point, and
\mathbf{w}
is the parameter.
We denote
\nabla f_{i}(\cdot)
and
\nabla g_{j}(\cdot)
as the gradient.
Then, the gradient for Eq.
229
is computed as

\nabla F(\bm{\theta})=\Big[\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\nabla f_{i}\Big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j=1}^{M}g_{j}(\mathbf{w})\Big)\Big]\Big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j=1}^{M}\nabla g_{j}(\mathbf{w})\Big),~\bm{\theta}=\{\mathbf{w}\},

(230)

where the dependency between inner- and outer-level sampling are not considered.
One can independently sample inner layer data to estimate
\tilde{g}\approx\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j=1}^{M}g_{j}(\mathbf{w})
and
\nabla\tilde{g}\approx\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j=1}^{M}\nabla g_{j}(\mathbf{w})
, sample outer layer data to estimate
\nabla\tilde{f}\approx\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\nabla f_{i}(\tilde{g})
, then estimate
\nabla F(\bm{\theta})
by using
[\nabla\tilde{f}]^{\top}\nabla\tilde{g}
.

By casting the optimizaion problem in GCN as composite optimization problem in Eq.
229
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{B}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}}}\text{Loss}(\mathbf{h}_{i}^{(L)},y_{i}),~\text{where}~\bm{\theta}=\{\mathbf{W}^{(1)}\},

(231)

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}^{(L)}

\displaystyle=\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(L)}\mathbf{X}\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(L)}),~\text{and}~\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(\ell)}=\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell-1)}\mathbf{X}\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(\ell-1)}),

which is different from the vanilla GCN model. To see this, we note that in vanilla GCNs, since the sampled nodes at the
\ell
th layer are dependent from the nodes sampled at the
(\ell+1)
th layer, we have
\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]=\mathbf{\mathbf{P}}^{(\ell)}\neq\mathbf{L}
. However in Eq.
231
, since the sampled nodes have no dependency on the weight matrices or nodes sampled at other layers, we can easily obtain
\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}^{(\ell)}]=\mathbf{L}
. These key differences makes the analysis more involved and are reflected in all three theorems, that give us different results.

Different gradient computation and algorithm.

The stochastic gradients to update the parameters in Eq.
231
are computed as

\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})}{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(\ell)}}=\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})}{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(L)}}\Big(\prod_{j=\ell+1}^{L}\frac{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(j)}}{\partial\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(j-1)}}\Big).

(232)

However in GCN, there are two types of gradient at each layer (i.e.,
\tilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}
and
\tilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}
) that are fused with each other (i.e.,
\tilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}
is a part of
\tilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell-1)}
and
\tilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}
is a part of
\tilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell-1)}
) but with different functionality.

\tilde{\mathbf{D}}^{(\ell)}
is passing gradient between different layers,
\tilde{\mathbf{G}}^{(\ell)}
is passing gradient to weight matrices.

These two types of gradient and their coupled relation make both algorithm and analysis different from
zhang2019multi
.
For example in
zhang2019multi
, the zeroth-order variance reduction is applied to
\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{t}^{(\ell)}
in Eq.
229
(please refer to Algorithm 3 in
zhang2019multi
), where
\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}
is used as a control variant to reduce the variance of
\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{t}^{(\ell)}
, i.e.,

\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{t}^{(\ell+1)}=\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{t-1}^{(\ell+1)}+\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{X}\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{t}^{(\ell)})-\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{X}\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}).

(233)

However in
SGCN++
, the zeroth-order variance reduction is applied to
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell)}
. Because the node sampled at the
t
th and
(t-1)
th iteration are unlikely the same, we cannot directly use
\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}
to reduce the variance of
\mathbf{H}^{(\ell)}_{t}
. Instead, the control variant in
SGCN++
is computed by applying historical weight
\mathbf{W}^{(\ell)}_{t-1}
on the historical node embedding from previous layer
\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t-1}
, i.e.,

\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t}^{(\ell)}=\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}+\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{(\ell)})-\sigma(\widetilde{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{(\ell)}\mathbf{H}^{(\ell-1)}_{t-1}\mathbf{W}_{t-1}^{(\ell)}).

(234)

These changes are not simply heuristic modifications, but all reflected in the analysis and the result.

Different theoretical results and intuition.

The aforementioned differences further result in a novel analysis of Theorem
4.1
, where we show that the vanilla sampling-based GCNs suffer a residual error
\Delta_{\mathbf{b}}
that is not decreasing as the number of iterations
T
increases, and this residual error is strongly connected to the difference between sampled and full Laplacian matrices. This is one of our novel observations for GCNs, when compared to (1) multi-level composite optimization with layerwise changing learning rate
yang2019multilevel
;
chen2020solving
, (2) variance reduction based methods
zhang2019multi
, and (3) the previous analysis on the convergence of GCNs
fastgcn
;
biased_but_consistent
.
Our observation can be used as a theoretical motivation on using first-order and doubly variance reduction, and can mathematically explain why VRGCN outperform GraphSAGE, even with fewer nodes during training.
Furthermore, as the algorithm and gradient computation are different, the theoretical results in Theorems
5.1
and
6.1
are also different.

Different mean-square error formulation.

Both
cong2020minimal
and ours are inspired by bias-variance decomposition, with a key difference in formulation.
Let denote
\mathbf{g}
as the stochastic gradient and
\nabla F(\bm{\theta})
as the full-batch gradient. Our formulation follows the standard definition of mean-square error of stochastic gradient, which is
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{g}-\nabla F(\bm{\theta})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{g}-\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}]-\nabla F(\bm{\theta})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]
, where
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{g}-\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]
is known as the variance and
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}]-\nabla F(\bm{\theta})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}]
is known as the bias. [5] treats
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}]
as the gradient computed using all neighbors with mini-batch sampling (Eq. 2 in [5]), which is different from ours. This difference results in a different analysis and theoretical results. Furthermore, [5] does not provide any further analysis (e.g., convergence) based on their observation of bias-variance decomposition.
